# Content sources: the landing-page collections

This guide follows the same order and grouping as the landing page: 24 content cards and 1394 registered content items. Archives, datasets and grouped packs count as items; their internal articles, books, map features and individual pages are not separate cards.

Local links open saved content or browser tools on the Ark. Publisher links identify the original sources and require upstream internet. Saved editions are snapshots; a blocked publisher check does not mean the installed copy is missing. Long inventories expand below. Outstanding gaps are listed separately from installed material.

Generated from `dataark.yaml` and the dashboard card composition by `tools/document_content_sources.py` whenever the web site is deployed. See [Manage Library](/library.html) for current installation and update state.

## 5. Maps & Navigation

U.S. and Caribbean offline street maps. Flood, parcel and topographic reference layers are Massachusetts-focused.

The street maps are not nautical charts or a routing service. The expandable list below identifies the major Caribbean island groups.

### Open this collection

- [All 50 U.S. states](/maps.html)
- [Caribbean islands, USVI & Sint Maarten](/maps.html?collection=caribbean)
- [Flood hazards](/library-files/maps/flood/index.html)
- [Property boundaries](/library-files/maps/parcels/index.html)
- [Topographic maps](/library-files/maps/topographic/index.html)
- [Collection and update status](/library-files/maps/index.html)
- [Field navigation guides](/library-files/family-resources/index.html#navigation-field)
- [Sources and coverage](/docs/20-Administration/OfflineReferences.html)

::: details All 50 U.S. states

The nationwide street-map archive covers all 50 states, including Alaska and Hawaii. This is one map dataset, not 50 separate downloads.

:::

::: details Caribbean island groups and destinations

The configured rectangular extraction spans longitude -89 to -58 and latitude 9 to 28, to zoom 15. It includes the following major island groups and adjoining coasts; this is a coverage overview, not an enumeration of every small island or a guarantee of feature completeness.

- Greater Antilles: Cuba, Hispaniola (Haiti and the Dominican Republic), Jamaica and Puerto Rico.
- Virgin Islands: U.S. Virgin Islands and British Virgin Islands.
- Northern/eastern Lesser Antilles: Anguilla, Sint Maarten / Saint Martin, Saint Barthelemy, Antigua and Barbuda, Saint Kitts and Nevis, Montserrat and Guadeloupe.
- Southern Lesser Antilles: Dominica, Martinique, Saint Lucia, Barbados, Saint Vincent and the Grenadines, Grenada, Trinidad and Tobago.
- Southern Caribbean: Aruba, Bonaire and Curacao.
- Other island groups: Bahamas, Turks and Caicos, Cayman Islands.

Bermuda is outside this extraction. The map viewer offers quick locations for Sint Maarten, USVI/BVI, Puerto Rico and the Bahamas.

:::

::: details Saved content (2 items)

- [All 50 U.S. states offline street map (October 22, 2026)](/library-files/maps/osm/usa.pmtiles) — [publisher](https://docs.protomaps.com/basemaps/downloads)
- [Caribbean islands offline street map (October 22, 2026)](/maps.html?collection=caribbean) — [publisher](https://docs.protomaps.com/basemaps/downloads)

:::

## 10. Games, Coloring & Activities

Offline games, drawing, PDF coloring, adult and holiday coloring, and printable paper patterns.



### Open this collection

- [Games, drawing & activities](/activities.html)
- [PDF coloring](/coloring.html)
- [Printable paper studio](/printables.html)

### Included tools and activities

- [Activities](/activities.html) — PDF coloring and drawing, memory pairs, tic-tac-toe, sliding puzzle, word hunt, and ideas for play away from a screen.
- [PDF coloring studio](/coloring.html) — Open a PDF, select a page, color or draw and save the result.
- [Paper studio](/printables.html) — Printable paper patterns, ready-made packs and everyday worksheets; A4 and US Letter. Available patterns: Square grid; Engineering grid (5 subdivisions); Dot grid; Cross grid; Isometric / triangular; Isometric dots; Hexagonal; Polar coordinates; Logarithmic (both axes); Semi-logarithmic; Ruled lines; Wide ruled (8.7 mm); Narrow ruled (6.35 mm); Handwriting guides; Double-line handwriting; Cornell lined notes; Cornell grid notes; Cornell dot notes; Music staves; Guitar tablature; Bass tablature; Calligraphy guides; Character-writing squares; Chess / checkers board; Go board (19 × 19); Undated monthly calendar; Story & drawing page; Blank page.

## 13. Laws, Regulations & Codes

Federal statutes, state and territorial constitutional snapshots, and clearly scoped legal references.

The state/territory collection is constitutional text (and the DC Home Rule Act/Charter), not a complete collection of every state statute, regulation or adopted building code. Massachusetts and local references have the narrower scope named in each title.

## 14. Radio & Communications

Emergency communications, amateur radio exam practice, callsign search and Part 97.



### Open this collection

- [Procedures, message forms & family plans](/library-files/family-resources/index.html#emergency-comms)
- [Practice exam questions](/radio-study.html)
- [Callsign search, distance & bearing](/callsigns.html)
- [FCC Part 97](/library-files/civic-reference/documents/fcc-part97.pdf)

### Included tools and activities

- [Radio question drills](/radio-study.html) — Technician, General and Amateur Extra question pools with practice and round results.
- [Offline callsign search](/callsigns.html) — FCC amateur licensee search, grid-square proximity, distance and bearing; snapshot/download information.

## Scope and maintenance

This guide documents registered collections and linked tools. The Community board is a separate local service, outside the content-source card count. USA.gov is an online-only reference and is not presented as an installed collection. Unregistered files and every article/book inside a ZIM are outside this item-level inventory. The wider [content-gap register](../20-Administration/ContentGaps.md) records acquisition work; use Manage Library and the catalogue source logs for current status.
